However, the softmax path often evaluates shifted-score exponentials in higher precision, forms a temporary probability block, and quantizes it before low-bit $PV$ multiplication. This exp-then-quantize path creates a mismatch between a high-precision probability producer and a low-bit matrix consumer. We propose EFQ-Softmax (Exp-Free Quantization for Softmax)","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-09T05:04:33Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_09721.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是低比特 attention 里一个常被忽略但越来越显眼的瓶颈：QK^T 和 PV 已经可以放到 FP8/FP4 矩阵引擎上跑，但 softmax 概率路径仍常常先在高精度里做 exp，再形成临时概率块，再量化给低比特 PV 使用。这样一来，attention 中间最关键的 producer-consumer 路径出现了精度和实现上的错配。作者要解决的不是一般意义上的 softmax 近似，而是如何直接从 shifted attention scores 生成可供低比特 PV 消费的概率操作数，同时保持 FlashAttention 式 online recurrence 的数值稳定和归一化一致性。","importance":"对基座模型研究的价值主要在推理效率而不是预训练方法本身。它针对 attention kernel 的中间概率生成路径做了结构性简化，减少高精度 exp 和临时概率 tile 的开销，这对长上下文 LLM、VLM、视频生成模型都相关。更具体地说，这篇工作说明低比特 attention 的瓶颈已经从 GEMM 逐步转移到 softmax probability path；如果矩阵乘已经足够快，剩下的 elementwise exp、临时存储和再量化就会变成新的热点。这个判断对做高效 serving、kernel co-design、甚至训练时的低精度 attention 研究都有启发。不过它更偏系统/算子层，不直接改变预训练范式。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 EFQ-Softmax，目标是把 shifted scores 直接映射成 block-scaled E2M1 概率操作数，绕过传统 exp-then-quantize 流程。作者强调“Exp-Free”并不是完全消灭所有指数运算，而是去掉当前 score block 上密集的 elementwise exponential；row-level historical rescaling 需要的指数项仍然保留，以兼容 online attention recurrence。具体做法分几步。先在每个 microscaling block 上根据 local maximum 选择 exponent-only scale；然后把 shifted scores 映射到一个 scale-normalized residual domain；再用单一 affine index rule 生成非负 E2M1 code。最终得到的是由 E2M1 code 加上 block-level E8M0 scale 组成的 MXFP4 probability operand。这个 operand 被同时用于 numerator update 和 denominator update，避免了“softmax 分子和分母近似方式不一致”的问题。方法设计上最关键的约束有两个：一是必须直接产出能喂给低比特 PV 的格式，而不是先近似 exp 再另做量化；二是必须保留 FlashAttention/online softmax 的 row-max update、历史重缩放、高精度累加和最终归一化。换句话说，它不是单点替换一个 exp approximation，而是把 softmax 中间表示重写成与低比特矩阵引擎对齐的形式。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen3-8B、Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 和 WAN2.2-TI2V-5B，说明作者想证明方法不只适用于纯文本。文本模型上，Qwen3-8B 的七任务平均分从 MXFP4 基线的 0.6749 提升到 0.6773，幅度不大，但至少说明直接 score-to-E2M1 没有带来质量回退。多模态模型上，Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 的九任务平均分从 0.7826 提升到 0.8000，且六个 referring-expression grounding split 全部更好，这比纯文本结果更显著。视频生成模型 WAN2.2 上，VBench 显示其 temporal consistency 和 visual quality 与 FP16、MXFP4 基线相当，说明该近似没有明显破坏生成质量。摘要里没有给出完整吞吐和延迟数字，但从问题设定看，作者的卖点应当是减少 dense exp、消除临时高精度概率块、降低 softmax 路径的带宽和算子开销。读全文时需要特别确认 kernel-level speedup、不同 block size/scale 选择的敏感性，以及在长序列下收益是否更明显。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看方法图，尤其是把传统 exp-then-quantize 路径和 EFQ 直接 score-to-E2M1 路径并排比较的那张图，几分钟就能抓住设计意图。然后看 online recurrence 相关公式，确认哪些部分被替换，哪些数值稳定机制被保留。再看实验表里三类模型的结果，重点不是绝对分数，而是“质量不降甚至略升”的证据是否稳定。预计 20–30 分钟能读完主线。若你做 kernel、量化或 serving，值得深挖 block scaling、E2M1 code 生成规则、以及 numerator/denominator 共用近似这一点；这几处决定了它为什么不是普通 softmax approximation。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific mismatch in low-bit attention. QK^T and PV can already run on FP8 or FP4 matrix engines, but the softmax probability path often still computes exponentials in higher precision, materializes a temporary probability tile, and only then quantizes it for low-bit PV. That leaves a high-precision producer feeding a low-bit consumer. The problem here is not generic softmax approximation; it is how to generate probability operands directly from shifted attention scores in a form usable by low-bit value multiplication, while preserving the numerical structure of online FlashAttention-style recurrence.","importance":"This is mainly an inference-efficiency contribution rather than a pretraining-method contribution. Its relevance comes from where the bottleneck is moving: once the two attention GEMMs are accelerated by low-bit hardware, the probability-generation path becomes a larger fraction of runtime and memory traffic. The paper makes that shift explicit and proposes a kernel-friendly redesign of the softmax middle path. That matters for long-context LLMs, multimodal models, and video generation systems. For researchers working on efficient training or serving, the broader lesson is that low-bit attention is no longer just about quantizing GEMMs; the softmax path itself needs to be co-designed with the target numeric format.","technical_approach":"$1e","experiments":"$1f","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the main pipeline figure comparing the conventional exp-then-quantize path against the direct score-to-E2M1 path. That gives the design intent immediately. Then read the equations around online recurrence to see exactly what is preserved and what is replaced. Finally, inspect the result tables across the three model families; the key question is not peak benchmark score but whether quality remains stable under the new probability-generation path. A 20–30 minute pass should be enough. If you work on kernels, quantization, or serving, spend extra time on the block-scaling rule, the affine E2M1 code generation, and the shared approximation for numerator and denominator updates, because those are the parts that make this more than a generic softmax approximation trick.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"关注底层算子优化的研究员可看，EFQ-Softmax 绕过 exp 直接生成 E2M1 格式，对极致压榨推理硬件算力有启发。","en":"Relevant for researchers focused on low-level operator optimization; EFQ-Softmax bypasses exp to directly generate E2M1 formats, offering insights for maximizing inference hardware utilization."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Haohui Han","Yuming Wan","Hongni Wang","Pengcheng Xie","Xiaodong Yan","Runqi You","Wencong Zhang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["low-bit attention","quantization","softmax","inference efficiency"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09721","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.09721","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"blog-david-noel-ng-0e3c86b4","title":"Teaching an M4 CPU to Tell Stories with XOR, Popcount and SDOT","abstract":"Introduction Language models are designed in linear algebra, PyTorch functions, and translated onto hardware afterwards. You pick an architecture, then you bend CUDA, Metal, or BLAS to your will to run it, hoping the compilers will do a good job with graph optimisation. An ARM core already contains a pile of very fast operations that nobody invented for language models. It compares floating-po...","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"blog","sources":["blog"],"published_at":"2026-09-09T22:00:00+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/blog-david-noel-ng-0e3c86b4.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"在不把“Transformer=矩阵乘”当作前提的情况下，如何把 CPU 指令集（XOR、popcount、INT8 SDOT 等）当作可学习的函数基元，让梯度下降直接搜索一套适配硬件的数据表示与网络结构，从而在极小内存占用下实现可用的 next-token 语言建模。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的启发点不在于给出可迁移的 SOTA 配方，而在于两件事：\n1) 把“表示切换”（FP16↔INT8↔bit-packed）当作架构的一部分，并且在训练时就把硬离散决策暴露给优化器，避免导出时质量断崖。这对离散化 tokenizer、二值/多值激活、以及 any-order/非 AR 范式里常见的离散变量训练都有共通价值。\n2) 评估指标选择：用 BPB（bits per byte）而不是 token-level CE，显式抵消 tokenizer 改动带来的虚假收益。这对做 tokenizer/压缩式建模的人很实用。\n整体更偏“高质量工程实验记录 + 可复现代码”，与大规模预训练 recipe 的直接关联有限，因此适合泛读。","technical_approach":"方法核心是“指令即层（instruction-as-layer）”的可微搜索/筛选流程：\n- 设计空间：把 ARM Neon 的若干原语暴露成可训练算子，覆盖数值路径（FP16 residual/归一化、小修正）、INT8 SDOT（注意力投影/点积类计算）、bit-packed 逻辑路径（比较得到 bit、路由与三输入逻辑、popcount 统计再回到数值域）。\n- 表示流：残差流保持 FP16；通过 FCMGE（浮点比较）把通道阈值化成 bit 特征；在 bit 域用 EXT/BSL/EOR/CNT 等实现“可学习的布尔前馈程序”；再用 CNT/SDOT 把 bit 统计量解码回数值，并用少量 FMLA 做数值修正。\n- 训练与导出一致性：不是 PTQ。阈值与布尔程序端到端训练；训练时就做 FP16 rounding，并在 hard forward 中执行硬 bit 决策；梯度用 straight-through/hybrid 近似，近似只存在于优化阶段，部署执行完全硬逻辑。\n- 选择机制：每次引入一个原语/结构变体，用固定数据顺序与 seed 训练 next-token loss，观察验证曲线；同时导出“原生二进制权重镜像”并在 M4 上测 tokens/s 与字节占用，任何一项不过关就停止。用三 seed 校准末端 BPB 方差，避免被噪声误导。\n这套流程本质是把硬件约束纳入 neural architecture search 的目标函数，但实现上更朴素：小规模、手工迭代、以曲线形状与端侧实测为准。","experiments":"数据与设置：TinyStories V2（Eldan & Li，GPT-4 生成版），训练文本 2.228GB，验证 22.5MB；byte-level BPE，词表 2048，训练侧约 5.03e8 tokens。\n模型与性能：5 层、宽度 512；部署 learned values 约 3.53M；权重镜像 3,265,024 bytes；权重 + 256-token KV 约 3.36MB（可驻留在 M4 性能核 L2）；端侧解码中位数 20,882 tokens/s。\n质量指标：在“原生二进制”上验证 slice 达到 0.8786 BPB，并与框架评估一致。\n消融/对比：作者尝试了更便宜的 XOR/BCAX 风格 cell、以及更强因子分解的 minterm cell 等，曲线基本无改善；minterm cell 在布尔核上约 1.96× 更慢（给出 523ns vs 1,025ns 的量级对比），dual3 变体在 1M bytes 预算下差约 0.023 BPB 且无成本优势。一个重要观察是小模型阶段“loss 与样本文字观感相关性可能为负”，因此用曲线与 BPB 做选择而不是看样例。","reading_guide":"建议按“动机→表示→训练一致性→评估指标”顺序读：\n- 先看 BPB 的定义与为什么不用 token-level CE（这决定了后面所有比较是否可信）。\n- 再看布尔 FFN 的构造：FCMGE 阈值化、4096 bit 特征、三输入逻辑层、CNT/SDOT 解码回数值，以及为什么要在训练时就做 hard decision + rounding。\n- 重点留意作者的实验筛选流程：固定数据顺序/seed、三 seed 校准、同时看验证曲线与端侧 tokens/s/字节占用。这部分对做“受硬件约束的预训练/蒸馏/量化共训”很有参考。\n泛读时间：30–45 分钟；如果要复现或迁移到别的指令集（AVX512/VNNI、RISC-V V 扩展），需要再花时间读代码与导出路径。","authors_note":"David Noel Ng（个人项目；GitHub: dnhkng）"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Instead of designing LMs in linear algebra and hoping compilers map them well to hardware, can we treat the CPU instruction set (XOR, popcount, INT8 SDOT, etc.) as the architectural primitive set and let gradient descent shape a language model around those instructions—achieving usable next-token modeling under a tiny memory footprint and high on-device throughput?","importance":"This is not a scalable pretraining recipe for frontier models, but it is a sharp demonstration of two ideas relevant to foundation-model research:\n- Representation switching as a first-class architectural tool (FP16 ↔ INT8 ↔ bit-packed), trained end-to-end so the model learns to place decisions where the hardware has cheap primitives.\n- Metric hygiene for tokenizer/representation changes: using BPB (bits per byte) rather than token-level cross-entropy to avoid “winning” by packing more characters per token.\nIt’s best read as a rigorous engineering exploration with reproducible artifacts, offering transferable lessons for quantization-aware training, discrete/boolean components, and hardware-constrained model design.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"Dataset/tokenization: TinyStories V2 (Eldan & Li, GPT-4 release), 2.228GB train and 22.5MB validation; lossless byte-level BPE with vocab size 2048, yielding ~503M training tokens.\nModel: 5 layers, width 512; ~3.53M deployed learned values; 3,265,024-byte weight image; weights + a full 256-token KV allocation total ~3.36MB (fits in the M4 performance core L2).\nPerformance/quality: median 20,882 tokens/s on-device; 0.8786 BPB on the same validation slice, matching framework evaluation.\nAblations: alternative boolean cells (XOR/BCAX-style, factorized minterm) did not improve the curve; the minterm cell was ~1.96× slower in the isolated boolean core (reported ~523ns vs ~1,025ns per call), and a shallow-wide dual3 variant was ~0.023 BPB worse at a 1MB budget with no cost advantage.","reading_guide":"Read it for the mechanics, not for benchmark chasing:\n1) The BPB rationale (why token-level CE is misleading when methodology: fixed-order training, curve-based selection, native export, and real device benchmarking.\nSkim time is ~30–45 minutes; deeper value comes from inspecting the code/export pipeline if you Convolutional Memory for Long-Context Reasoning","abstract":"While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities, they often struggle with extremely long contexts due to fixed context limits. To address this, sequential approaches like MemAgent extend the effective context by reading text in segments and iteratively updating a fixed-size memory. However, this sequential paradigm suffers from high latency and requires costly reinforcement learning (RL) training, which can lead to overfitting on specific datasets. To overcome these ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-09T16:53:02Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_10441.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很现实的问题：当输入上下文远超模型原生窗口时，怎样在不重新训练模型、也不引入串行 memory agent 延迟的前提下，做相对可靠的长文档推理。作者针对的是两类现有路线的共同短板。第一类是直接扩上下文窗口，计算和延迟都很重，而且长上下文下常见 lost-in-the-middle，模型并不真正稳定利用远处证据。第二类是 MemAgent 一类顺序读文档、逐步更新记忆的方案，复杂度虽然线性，但推理路径是严格串行的，吞吐差；同时这类方法常依赖 RL 学记忆策略，容易在训练分布上学到捷径，回答看起来对，实际并没有忠实依赖给定上下文。ConvMem要解决的核心，就是把“长文档压缩+证据汇聚+最终回答”改写成一种可并行、层次化、训练无关的推理流程，并尽量减少顺序记忆链带来的误差累积和分布过拟合。","importance":"对基座研究的意义不在于它提出了新的预训练目标，而在于它给出了一种很清楚的 inference-time 结构化归纳偏置：把冻结 LLM 当作语义卷积核，用树形汇聚替代链式记忆更新。这个想法对长上下文系统设计有两点启发。其一，很多所谓“长上下文能力”问题，不一定要继续押注更长 attention，也可以通过外部层次化读写结构来补。其二，作者明确指出 RL-based memory agent 的一个风险：模型可能借助参数记忆和数据集模式完成回答，而不是忠实读取上下文。这一点对 post-train 与 long-context agent 的接口很重要，因为如果训练信号鼓励的是最终答案而不是证据忠实性，系统很容易学成 benchmark-specific policy。量级上，这篇工作更像一个有工程启发的推理框架，而不是会直接改写预训练范式的结果。对做长上下文 serving、memory scaffold、test-time compute 设计的人有参考价值；对纯 pretrain recipe 的直接影响较弱。","technical_approach":"方法的核心是把长文档问答写成分层卷积。给定问题Q和超长文档D，作者把冻结 LLM 视为一个“语义卷积核”：它接收一个文本窗口和问题提示，输出该窗口相对于问题的摘要、证据或中间记忆。第一层先把文档切成长度为W的窗口；每个窗口都可并行送入同一个 kernel 处理。随后把这些局部摘要再按层次继续聚合，形成树状结构，直到顶层得到可供最终回答的全局记忆。这样推理深度从顺序 agent 的 O(N) 链式步数，变成近似 O(log N) 的层次深度，代价是每层需要更多并行调用。\n\n作者围绕这个主干加了三个机制。第一是 configurable strides。若 stride S < W，就形成重叠窗口，同一段证据会被多个感受野重复扫描。这样做不是为了卷积意义上的平移不变性，而是为了缓解文本边界截断和单次摘要的随机性。长文档 QA 里证据常跨 chunk 边界，重叠扫描能提高召回。第二是 semantic skip connections。层次摘要会丢细节，尤其是实体、数字、关系链中的局部约束。作者让高置信原始证据绕过中间压缩层，直接传到最终回答阶段，类似残差保真。第三是 multi-kernel convolution。复杂问题先被分解成多个子问题，每个子问题对应一个独立 kernel 或语义通道，分别抽取相关证据，最后再汇总。这相当于显式减少不同推理线索之间的干扰。\n\n从方法定位看，ConvMem不是训练一个新模型，而是一个 prompt-and-orchestrate 框架。它依赖底座 LLM 已有的摘要、证据提取和问答能力。与 MemAgent 的关键区别有三点：一是没有显式可学习 memory policy；二是信息流是树而不是链；三是并行性来自同层 chunk 独立处理，而不是一步一步读完整篇文档。与直接 RAG 也不同，ConvMem不是先检索少量块再回答，而是对全篇做层次化覆盖式扫描，因此更接近“全局压缩推理”而不是“局部召回”。","experiments":"实验主要放在 RULER-HotpotQA 和 RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA 这类长上下文多跳问答设置上，目标是比较训练无关方法与 RL 训练的 memory agent。全文给出的主结论有三层。第一，ConvMem 在 training-free baselines 上有稳定优势，说明层次化并行汇聚比单次摘要、简单 map-reduce 或朴素分块问答更适合多跳证据整合。第二，在 out-of-distribution 评测上，ConvMem 的泛化好于 RL-trained memory agent。作者强调后者在分布内成绩可能更高，但换数据后容易掉得明显，且会出现“答案像是对的，但证据并不来自输入文档”的现象。第三，三个结构组件都有效：重叠 stride 提升证据召回，skip connection 改善细节保留，多 kernel 对复杂问题更有帮助。\n\n从全文可读出的实验重点，不是绝对分数刷新多少，而是对 failure mode 的拆解。作者专门分析了 RL agent 的过拟合：当问题与预训练知识或训练集模式有重合时，agent 可能直接依赖参数记忆生成 plausible answer，而不是沿着 memory state 忠实推理。ConvMem因为不训练 policy，反而少了这类 benchmark-specific shortcut。若精读实验部分，建议重点看：1）ID 与 OOD 的分开汇报；2）不同 stride、是否 overlap 的消融；3）是否加入 skip evidence 的消融；4）单 kernel 与多 kernel 的对比；5）延迟或并行深度分析。摘要里没有完整数字，全文片段也未给出所有表格数值，因此这里不虚构具体百分点。但从作者组织实验的方式看，他们更想证明“并行层次结构+训练无关”在泛化和忠实性上有优势，而不是单纯追求某个 benchmark 的最高分。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不必逐行抠公式。先看 Introduction，抓住作者要替代的是哪类方法：不是长 attention，而是 RL memory agent。然后看方法部分里对 W、S、L、C 四个量的定义，以及 semantic kernel 的 prompt 设计，这决定了框架到底是怎么把 CNN 类比落到文本推理上的。接着重点看三个机制：overlap stride、semantic skip、multi-kernel。这里最值得想的是，它们分别对应长文档推理中的哪种错误：边界截断、摘要丢细节、不同推理线索互相污染。\n\n实验部分建议优先看 OOD 结果和作者对 RL overfitting 的案例分析，而不是只看主表排名。如果时间有限，30 到 40 分钟足够把方法和主要结论读明白。若要深挖，可以追问三个问题：第一，ConvMem 的收益有多少来自更好的 prompt engineering，而不是结构本身；第二，树形汇聚在更长链式推理上会不会过早压缩掉关键中间变量；第三，这种框架能否和可训练的 selector / verifier 结合，而不重新引入 RL 过拟合问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a practical long-context reasoning problem: how to answer questions over inputs far beyond the model’s native context window without retraining the model and without paying the latency cost of sequential memory agents. The target gap is between two existing families. Enlarged-context models remain expensive at inference and often degrade as context grows. Sequential memory agents such as MemAgent reduce complexity to linear-time streaming, but they introduce a hard serial bottleneck and typically rely on RL to learn memory-update policies. The authors argue that this RL setup can overfit to benchmark distributions and produce answers that look correct while not being faithfully grounded in the provided document. ConvMem is proposed as a training-free alternative that turns long-document reasoning into a hierarchical, parallel aggregation process.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model researchers is not a new pretraining objective, but a clean systems-level inductive bias for inference-time long-context reasoning. The key idea is to treat a frozen LLM as a semantic convolution kernel and replace chain-structured memory updates with tree-structured aggregation. That matters for two reasons. First, it suggests that some long-context failures should be addressed by external hierarchical read-compress-reason scaffolds rather than only by pushing attention windows further. Second, the paper makes a useful point about RL-trained memory agents: if optimization rewards final answers more than evidence faithfulness, the policy can exploit parametric knowledge and dataset regularities instead of actually reading the context. So the contribution is more about long-context orchestration, faithfulness, and generalization under distribution shift than about changing the core pretraining paradigm.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"$22","reading_guide":"$23","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"把“长文档推理”从线性rollout改成对数深度树聚合，工程直觉对；但是否真比RAG/直接长上下文更干净，要盯住token预算与证据保真度对比。","en":"The log-depth tree aggregation is a solid engineering idea for long-context latency, but treat claims as weak until you check token/call budgets and evidence-faithfulness vs RAG and native long-context."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Hongming Zhang","Zhaozhen Gu","Fengshuo Bai","Ming Hao","Qingyang Zhang","Yuanyuan Wang","Shiyang Tang","Yanna Wang","Bo Xu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["long-context","hierarchical-aggregation","inference-time","memory-augmented-llm","parallelization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10441","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.10441","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.10346","title":"Beyond One-Size-Fits-All: Sample-Adaptive Strategy Routing for Vision Token Pruning in MLLMs","abstract":"Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) process hundreds or thousands of visual tokens per image, incurring prohibitive inference costs. While existing vision token pruning methods mitigate this overhead, they implicitly assume that a single fixed pruning strategy can be applied uniformly across all inputs. Our analysis further reveals that ranking pruning methods by average benchmark accuracy conceals substantial sample-wise complementarity: although the average-best strategy excels overall, a","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-09T15:44:32Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_10346.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决 MLLM 推理中的视觉 token 裁剪选择问题：现有方法通常对所有样本使用同一种裁剪策略，但不同样本适合的策略并不相同，平均最优的策略在很多单样本上不是最优选择。","importance":"这是一篇推理效率论文，对预训练研究的间接启发在于视觉 token 的冗余并不是固定分布，而是和图像内容、问题文本、任务类型、裁剪比例共同相关。对 native multimodal pretraining 或视觉 tokenizer 设计来说，这个观察有意义：视觉 token 压缩不应只被看作统一的后处理，也可能需要 query-conditioned 或 task-conditioned 的动态机制。它也提示评测压缩方法时不能只看平均准确率，否则会掩盖样本级互补性。","technical_approach":"论文提出 VIP-Router，一个轻量级、可插拔的视觉裁剪策略路由器。它不重新设计裁剪算法，而是在多个已有候选策略之间做 sample-adaptive selection。候选包括 Full、FastV、VisionZip、PruMerge+、DART，其中 Full 保留全部视觉 token，作为不适合裁剪时的安全选项。模型由三部分组成：冻结的视觉和文本 preview encoder，text-to-vision cross-attention 模块，以及 ratio-conditioned MLP utility predictor。preview encoder 提供低成本的图像和文本表征；cross-attention 提取和问题相关的视觉信息；utility predictor 在给定保留比例下预测每个候选策略的效用。推理时只执行预测分数最高的策略，因此不会因为试跑多个裁剪方法而抵消节省的计算量。","experiments":"实验主要在 VTC-Bench Group A 上进行。这个设置只保留“全 token 能答对、等比例图像下采样会答错”的样本，因此更能测试视觉 token 裁剪是否破坏关键信息。论文发现，若至少有一种裁剪策略能答对，超过三分之一的样本是 Best Fixed 策略失败、其他策略成功，说明样本级互补性真实存在。VIP-Router 在五个 reduction ratio 上都超过最佳固定裁剪策略。按实际 token 成本计算，平均 utility 从 30.88 提升到 37.68，相对提升 22.0%；平均准确率从 40.35% 提升到 51.19%，相对提升 26.9%。路由器约 1.4M 可训练参数，只占底层 MLLM 参数量约 0.017%。论文还报告它可迁移到不同 MLLM backbone，并对未见 benchmark 有一定 zero-shot transfer。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中关于样本级互补性的分析图，这是论文成立的关键；再看方法部分的 routing objective，尤其是为什么把 Full 作为候选，以及如何用 cost-aware 目标排序压缩策略；最后看主实验中不同 reduction ratio 的 accuracy 和 utility。若做 MLLM serving 或视觉 token 压缩，可以细看消融实验，判断 preview feature、cross-attention 和 ratio conditioning 分别贡献多少。若关注纯语言模型预训练，可只读动机和实验结论。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses strategy selection for vision token pruning in MLLM inference. Existing pruning methods usually apply one fixed strategy to all inputs, but the best pruning strategy varies across samples. A method that is best on average can still be the wrong choice for many individual examples.","importance":"This is primarily an inference-efficiency paper, with indirect relevance to multimodal pretraining and visual tokenization. The key signal is that visual-token redundancy is input-dependent and query-dependent. Compression behavior depends on the image, the question, the task, and the pruning ratio. This matters for native multimodal models because token selection may need to be conditioned on the linguistic objective rather than treated as a static visual preprocessing step. It also cautions against evaluating token pruning only by average benchmark accuracy.","technical_approach":"The proposed method, VIP-Router, is a lightweight plug-in router that selects among existing pruning strategies on a per-sample basis. It does not modify the underlying MLLM or the pruning algorithms. The candidate set includes Full, FastV, VisionZip, PruMerge+, and DART. Full-token inference is kept as an explicit option when pruning is predicted to be unsafe. The router uses frozen visual and textual preview encoders, a text-to-vision cross-attention module, and a ratio-conditioned MLP utility predictor. The preview encoders provide low-cost representations. Cross-attention extracts query-conditioned visual evidence. The MLP predicts the utility of each candidate strategy for a given retained-token ratio. At inference time, only the top-scoring option is executed, avoiding the cost of running multiple pruning methods.","experiments":"The main experiments use VTC-Bench Group A, where full-token inference is correct but equivalent-ratio image downsampling fails. This setup stresses pruning-sensitive examples. The paper reports that among samples where at least one pruning strategy succeeds, more than one third are cases where the best fixed strategy fails but another strategy succeeds. VIP-Router outperforms the best fixed pruning strategy across five reduction ratios. Accounting for realized token cost, average utility improves from 30.88 to 37.68, a 22.0% relative gain. Average accuracy improves from 40.35% to 51.19%, a 26.9% relative gain. The router adds about 1.4M trainable parameters, roughly 0.017% of the MLLM parameter count. The paper also reports applicability across different MLLM backbones and positive zero-shot transfer to unseen benchmarks.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Start with the analysis of sample-level complementarity in the introduction, since it is the main justification for routing. We ask whether any such rule is both safe and token-saving, and whether one can be selected once and reused. a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-09T10:15:31Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_09989.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论的是 reasoning model 的 early exit 信号是否可靠。更具体地说，它检验一种很自然的想法：在一条尚未结束的推理轨迹上，反复插入 probe 去问“当前答案是什么”，如果最近几次 probe 给出相同答案，就提前停止生成。作者要回答两个问题：这种 self-consensus 规则能不能同时做到安全和省 token；以及是否存在一个可以跨模型、跨 benchmark 复用的通用规则。全文给出的结论是否定的。问题不在阈值没调好，而在于 consensus 测到的是“当前被 probe 诱导出的答案是否持续”，不是“推理是否已经完成”。","importance":"它和预训练的直接关系不如训练方法论文强，但对做长链推理模型、test-time compute、推理轨迹分析的人有明确启发。第一，论文把一个常见误区讲清楚：稳定输出不等于内部计算已经收敛。这个区分对任何把生成轨迹当作可观测推理状态的人都重要。第二，它的方法学很扎实：在 frozen trajectories 上离线重放 probe，并把 probe token 计入成本，避免了“因为在线干预改变了原始轨迹”或“省下主 decode token 却没算 probe 开销”的常见评估偏差。第三，它说明 early exit 的关键不是找一个更激进的 consensus 阈值，而是寻找更接近 reasoning boundary 的信号。作者拿 DEER 作为对照，证明问题出在信号本身，而不是 early exit 这个方向本身。","technical_approach":"技术路线分两部分。第一部分是机制诊断，目标是证明存在 consensus–termination gap。作者在一条固定的主推理轨迹上截取不同前缀，对每个前缀离线追加 probe，提取“当前答案”。这里强调 frozen trajectories 很重要，因为这样不同规则看到的是同一条底层推理，不会因为 probe 改写后续生成。围绕这个设置，作者做了三类直接测量：\n1）措辞敏感性测试：对同一个 frozen prefix，用不同 wording 的 probe 重新提问，观察提取出的“当前答案”是否变化。如果早期前缀对 probe wording 很敏感，就说明所谓当前答案更多是被问出来的，而不是模型内部已经稳定持有的结论。\n2）错误停止人工标注：对 stopped-but-wrong 的案例做人工分类，区分“模型其实已经收敛到错误答案”与“模型尚未收敛，只是 probe 暂时逼出了一个占位答案”。\n3）非终止答案承诺率：统计 consensus stop 时提交的答案，后续原始轨迹是否会离开这个答案；如果会离开，而且后来改对了，那就说明 early exit 切掉的是纠错计算。\n\n第二部分是大规模规则搜索。作者预注册了一个 windowed consensus 家族：窗口大小、同意比例阈值以及若干 operational knobs 组合成 3,520 条规则。在两种模型、三个 benchmark 的 frozen trajectories 上做 problem-grouped split，先在开发集上按预先固定的 acceptance gates 选规则，再看是否能迁移到 held-out split 和未见模型。这里的设计很像严谨的模型选择实验：不是展示几个手工挑的 operating point，而是问整个规则家族里是否存在真正可用的点。作为对照，作者把非 consensus 的 DEER 也放进同一套 pipeline 和 token accounting 中比较，从而把“signal 不行”和“任务本身不可能”区分开。","experiments":"实验结果很集中，也很有说服力。先看机制层面的证据。对同一个 frozen prefix 换 probe wording，轨迹前 10% 位置有 54% 的概率得到不同答案，而接近结尾时这个比例降到 16%。这说明早期所谓“当前答案”高度依赖提问方式。对 134 个 wrong-stop 案例的人工标注显示，超过一半属于“模型尚未真正收敛，只是被 probe 逼出一个答案”，而只有约四分之一属于“模型已经稳定但稳定错了”。再看非终止答案承诺：在一个仍能节省 32% token 的 consensus 规则下，每 9 次停止就有 1 次提交了后续轨迹会放弃的答案；在这 216 个 non-terminal stop 里，155 个阻断了后续本可发生的纠错，而只有 16 个是提前锁定了一个最终会被模型自己改坏的正确答案。也就是说，被切掉的计算主要是有用的纠错计算。\n\n规则搜索的结果更直接。作者对 3,520 条 consensus 规则做预注册 sweep，没有任何一条能在开发集上同时通过预设的安全和节省门槛，因此没有一条被带到后续测试。相反，在训练划分上却有 478 条规则能过门槛，说明如果只报单一 split 或只挑好看的点，很容易得出过于乐观的结论。作者还报告 frontier 在 test split 上复现性很高（r = 0.98），并在未见模型上也保持趋势，说明“没有好规则”不是偶然噪声。更关键的是，对照方法 DEER 在同样 protocol 下能过所有门槛：在未见模型上，最低只损失 0.3 个百分点准确率，就能节省 28% token。匹配节省率比较时，consensus 的代价明显更高：在 28% net saving 下，最便宜的 consensus 规则仍损失 6.0 个百分点准确率；在 33% saving 下损失 8.3 个百分点，而 DEER 分别只损失 0.5 和 2.0 个百分点。作者还提到一个已部署 operating point，CertaIndex (CoT)，在同样轨迹上会损失 56 到 70 个百分点，说明这类 heuristic 在严格评估下风险很大。","reading_guide":"这篇虽然标成 skim，但如果你关心 reasoning inference，建议读得比普通 skim 更仔细。先看引言，把作者要反驳的对象界定清楚：不是 self-consistency，而是对单条部分轨迹做 repeated probing 的 self-consensus。然后直接看 Section 4，这里是全文最有信息量的部分。重点看三类证据：probe wording sensitivity、wrong-stop taxonomy、non-terminal committed answers。它们共同支撑“agreement 只说明答案在当前 probe 下持续，不说明推理终止”。接着看 Section 5 的 preregistered sweep，尤其是 acceptance gates、problem-grouped split、frozen trajectory replay 和 token accounting 规则。这些实验设计细节决定了结论的可信度。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看 Figure 1 和与 28%/33% saving 对应的 accuracy trade-off，再看 54% vs 16%、155 vs 16 这些关键数字。预计 30 分钟能抓住主结论；如果你在做 early exit、verifier、reasoning boundary detection，建议完整读完实验和附录中的 protocol 细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether self-consensus is a reliable early-exit signal for reasoning models. The setup is simple: take a partially generated reasoning trajectory, repeatedly probe it for its current answer, and stop once recent probes agree. The authors ask two stricter questions than prior work: can any such rule be both safe and token-saving, and can one operating point be selected once and reused across models and benchmarks? Their answer is no. The issue is not poor threshold tuning. It is that consensus measures persistence of a probe-elicited answer, not completion of the underlying reasoning process.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to anyone working on reasoning models, test-time compute, or trajectory-level analysis. First, it makes an important conceptual distinction: stable extracted answers are not the same thing as terminated reasoning. That matters whenever generated text is treated as a faithful readout of latent computation. Second, the evaluation methodology is unusually careful. All probing is replayed offline on frozen trajectories, and probe tokens are charged in the budget, which removes two common sources of optimism in early-exit work: changing the trajectory by intervening online, and claiming savings without counting probe overhead. Third, the paper shows that the bottleneck is the signal, not early exit as a goal. A non-consensus control, DEER, succeeds under the same protocol, so the negative result is targeted and informative.","technical_approach":"$24","experiments":"$25","reading_guide":"$26","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇该看，因为它把“答案稳定=推理结束”这个常见偷换拆得很干净；若你在做 early-exit 或 test-time scaling，只看 abstract 加实验主表就足够改 prior。","en":"Worth reading because it cleanly separates answer stability from reasoning completion; if you work on early exit or test-time scaling, the abstract plus main result table is enough to update your prior."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yunxiang Mo","Donghao Zhao","Hejia Geng"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["early-exit","reasoning","evaluation-protocol","test-time-compute","self-consensus"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09989","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.09989","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.10226","title":"$$Φ$-Bench: Can Large Language Models Engineer the Infrastructure That Powers Them?","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in reasoning and code generation, raising the prospect that they could assist in developing and optimizing the very infrastructure that powers them. By calibrating item difficulty for 1,000 open-weights language models over 14,042 MMLU test items using Item Response Theory, we show that evaluating both abilities via a single test is inherently flawed. Difficulty is then regressed on a deterministic, text-extr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-08T19:11:10Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_09372.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["math.NT","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“MMLU好不好用”，而是更具体的问题：把 57 个学科、14042 道题压成一个总分，这个总分到底在测什么。作者用心理测量学的语言把问题拆开：MMLU 是否真能作为单一“通用能力”量表，还是主要在测事实检索能力，同时把程序性推理和知识回忆混在一起。全文的关键点在于，它不是凭直觉说“MMLU偏知识”，而是先用 1000 个开源模型、约 1400 万条作答记录拟合 2PL IRT，得到每道题的 difficulty 和 discrimination，再把题目难度回归到一组可从文本中确定性提取的结构特征上，检验“知识负荷”和“推理负荷”是否真是同一个潜变量的不同表面形式。结论是否定的：MMLU 的 aggregate score 不能稳定代表单一能力，且更贴近 factual retrieval。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在评测解释层，而不是提出新训练方法。它直接影响两类常见做法。第一，用 MMLU 总分做模型能力校准、scaling 对比、recipe 选择时，容易把“记住了多少知识”误读成“推理更强”。第二，用 MMLU 作为训练后改进或中期训练策略的目标时，优化方向可能被 benchmark 的测量偏差牵着走。贡献量级上，这不是一个新 benchmark，而是对现有主流 benchmark 的测量学审计。价值在于把“benchmark score 的可解释性”从经验判断推进到可检验的统计结构：单维能力假设是否成立、不同分区是否测同一构念、文本结构特征与题目难度的映射是否跨域稳定。对预训练研究员来说，最有用的启发是：以后看 MMLU 提升，尤其要问提升来自知识覆盖、题目模板适配，还是推理过程本身。","technical_approach":"方法分三层。第一层是 IRT 标定。作者用 1000 个 open-weight 模型在 MMLU 全测试集上的二元作答矩阵，拟合 Two-Parameter Logistic model。相比 Joint Maximum Likelihood，他们明确选择 NumPyro/JAX 上的 SVI 来避免 incidental parameters problem，因为模型能力参数数量随样本数增长，JML 下 item 参数会不一致。这里的输出是每个 item 的 difficulty b_i 和 discrimination a_i，以及每个模型的 ability θ_j。第二层是解释性难度建模。作者构造了一套 deterministic、text-extractable 的结构复杂度框架，用来区分 declarative retrieval demand 和 procedural reasoning load。文中点名的核心指标包括 Zipf Rarity、Entity Density，以及 Weighted Symbolic Complexity Graph（WSCG）。前两者近似题目对稀有知识和实体密度的依赖，后者刻画符号关系和顺序推理负荷。作者不是做黑箱 embedding 回归，而是坚持可解释、可复现的文本结构特征。第三层是测量不变性与维度检验。作者利用 benchmark 设计者原本给出的 STEM / non-STEM 分区，拟合 confirmatory two-dimensional IRT，让每道题只加载到所属分区的潜变量上，再比较它与单维模型的 held-out 预测表现，并检验结构特征到难度的映射在两个分区中是否相同。统计上用了 joint Wald test、subject-clustered covariance、wild cluster bootstrap 和 Holm correction，说明作者很在意“显著性是否只是学科聚类造成的假象”。全文最重要的技术判断是：如果一个 benchmark 真测单一能力，那么 item difficulty 对结构特征的响应关系应在不同子域上近似不变；而 MMLU 不满足这一点。","experiments":"实验规模相当扎实。模型侧覆盖 1000 个开源模型，去重后为 869 个 distinct checkpoints、50 个 base-family-and-size 组合，参数规模从 3.2M 到 180B+。题目侧使用 MMLU 全量 14042 题，因此总观测约 1400 万。IRT 标定后，item difficulty 范围为 [-5.00, 7.12]，标准差 2.24；模型 ability 范围为 [-2.02, 3.14]，标准差 1.03。作者把 1000 个模型随机分成两个 500 模型 cohort 分别标定，得到 difficulty 向量相关性 r_hh = 0.9921，说明题目难度估计很稳。核心结果有四个。其一，二维 IRT 优于单维模型，且 STEM 与 non-STEM 两个潜在能力的相关系数 ρ = 0.965，显著低于 1，posterior mass 在 0.99 以上为 0，说明“几乎一样”但不是“同一个东西”。其二，等长条件下去衰减后的 subscale correlation 为 0.977，也支持两者高度相关但不可互换。其三，文本结构到难度的映射在两个分区不具不变性；九个对比里，只有 Entity Density 在 subject-clustered covariance、wild cluster bootstrap 和 Holm 多重校正后仍显著，而且方向在 STEM 与 non-STEM 中不同：在 STEM 里更像 active constraint，在 non-STEM 里更像 retrieval cue。其四，aggregate rank 与 non-STEM accuracy 的相关更强，即使把两个分区做 test length equalization 之后仍如此。这一点很关键，因为它说明总分偏向 non-STEM 所代表的能力成分，而不是简单由题量不平衡造成。引言还引用了 CoT 在 MMLU 上收益有限、在 MMLU-Pro 上收益更大的既有观察，全文则把这种经验现象落到了可量化的测量结构上。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但最好别只看摘要。建议先看 Introduction，作者把问题定义得很清楚：不是争论“推理是否存在”，而是问 benchmark 的单分数解释是否合法。然后重点看 Methodology 里 2PL IRT 的设定、为什么不用 JML、结构特征框架各指标分别对应什么认知负荷。接着看二维 IRT 与单维 IRT 的比较结果，以及测量不变性检验那部分，这是全文真正的证据链。图表上优先关注：item difficulty / model ability 分布、双维潜变量相关、结构特征回归系数在 STEM 与 non-STEM 的差异、aggregate rank 与两个分区准确率的关系。预计 30–45 分钟能抓住主结论。如果要深挖，值得想两个问题：第一，作者用 STEM / non-STEM 作为预定义边界是合理但粗糙的，若换成更细的 subject clustering，单维假设会不会进一步崩；第二，结构特征框架仍是文本表层可提取特征，它能解释“为什么难”，但未必完全等同于模型内部的知识/推理分解，这里还有继续做 representation-level validation 的空间。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise measurement question: when MMLU compresses 57 subjects and 14,042 items into one aggregate score, what latent ability is that score actually measuring? The authors test whether MMLU can be treated as a coherent one-dimensional scale of general capability, or whether it mainly reflects factual retrieval while conflating it with procedural reasoning. The full paper is stronger than the abstract because it does not stop at the claim that MMLU is “knowledge-heavy.” It first calibrates item difficulty and discrimination with a 2PL IRT model over roughly 14 million responses from 1,000 open-weight models, then regresses calibrated difficulty on deterministic text-structural features designed to separate retrieval demand from inferential load. The central finding is that the aggregate score is not a stable measure of a single construct, and it leans toward factual retrieval more than reasoning.","importance":"Its value is in benchmark interpretation rather than a new training method. For pretraining work, this matters in two common settings. First, MMLU is often used to compare scaling trends, recipes, and model families. If the benchmark is retrieval-weighted, gains can be misread as reasoning gains. Second, if MMLU is used as a target when tuning mid-training or post-training strategies, optimization may be steered by the benchmark’s measurement bias rather than by the intended capability. The contribution is an audit of a widely used benchmark through psychometric structure: whether a unidimensional ability assumption holds, whether benchmark partitions measure the same latent trait, and whether the mapping from text structure to item difficulty is invariant across domains. For a foundation-model researcher, the practical takeaway is simple: when MMLU moves, ask whether the gain comes from broader knowledge coverage, benchmark-specific pattern matching, or actual improvements in multi-step reasoning.","technical_approach":"$27","experiments":"$28","reading_guide":"$29","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读 IRT 和 STEM/non-STEM 分解；它会改变用 MMLU 聚合分数选预训练 checkpoint 的可信度。","en":"Read the IRT and STEM/non-STEM breakdown; it weakens the case for using aggregate MMLU as a checkpoint-selection signal."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Dana Paquin","Riddhiman Jain"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["MMLU","evaluation","psychometrics","IRT","benchmark-audit"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09372","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.09372","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.10092","title":"RAP: Research Attention Prediction Reveals Target-Conditioned Evidence Acquisition Biases","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as research agents, yet their ability to track shifts in research attention is difficult to evaluate because reviews and research ideas lack uniquely verifiable outcomes. We introduce Research Attention Prediction (RAP), a rolling benchmark covering 278 AI/ML fields and 1,390 episodes. At each cut-off, an LLM agent searches a temporally restricted arXiv corpus and predicts the next six months' paper shares across eight frozen research directions. 诊断。它把“研究助手是否理解领域趋势”从主观综述质量，转成带时间边界、可回放、可计量的分布预测任务。对训练数据研究也有参考意义：模型失败不只是因为预测头不准，还因为 forecast prompt 改变了检索策略，使模型在需要近期信号时反而少拿近期证据。这提示训练 research agent label，再经过规范化、原型匹配和保守合并，得到重叠的 AI/ML operational fields；保留 2026-01-01 前至少有 300 个 paper-field membership 的领域。最终有 278 个有效领域、1,390 个 episode。每个领域使用 pre-2024 证据构造固定的 8 个方向 codebook，方向包含名称、定义、纳入/排除边界和早期例子。每个 rolling origin 下，agent 只能访问截止日前文献，并预测未来 6 个月新论文在 8 个方向上的份额。论文比较 Closed、Fixed-window Search、Expanding-history Search，并加入 State query：先让 agent 描述当前状态，再用状态 carry-forward 作为 persistence control。还使用 frozen-evidence replay、exact-history intervention 和 outcome-aligned finetuning 来分离三类能力：证据获取、状态恢复、未来更新。","experiments":"RAP 覆盖 278 个 AI/ML 领域、5 个半年 rolling origins、1,390 个预测 episode，候选语料规模为 356,357 篇论文。主要结果是：Search 通常优于 Closed，但 4 个诊断模型的自然 agent 条件都没有超过基于精确计数的 EWMA baseline。这个 EWMA baseline 使用指数加权移动平均，直接利用历史论文份额，是强 persistence 基线。更细的诊断显示，在 Expanding-history 条件下，State carry-forward 对 4 个模型都优于直接 Forecast，说明让模型“预测未来”并不一定比先恢复当前状态再延续更好。frozen-evidence replay 进一步发现 acquisition bias：Forecast 导向的检索策略会降低最近 6 个月证据的占比，而近期证据恰好对预测未来更有用。给 exact history 后，future-specific updating 仍有限；只有 GPT-5.5 在 reopened Search 条件下略微超过 EWMA。outcome-aligned finetuning 可以让 Qwen3-4B 在依赖不重叠的 Test fields 后期 episode 上提升 +0.105，说明任务可学习，但没有证明已修复检索阶段的偏差。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 25–35 分钟。先看 benchmark construction：field carry-forward 优于 Forecast 的结果，这是论文最有信息量的发现。finetuning 结果可快速看，重点是 +0.105 的改善说明 RAP 可作为训练任务，但当前 evidence acquisition failure 还没有被机制性解决。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper introduces Research Attention Prediction, a rolling benchmark for testing whether LLM research agents can use temporally restricted arXiv evidence to predict how the next six months of papers in an AI/ML field will be distributed across eight fixed research directions.","importance":"The contribution is mainly an evaluation and diagnosis framework for research agents, not a pretraining recipe. Its relevance to foundation-model research lies in the separation between evidence acquisition and final forecasting. The main failure is not only weak numerical forecasting; forecast-oriented prompting changes what the agent retrieves. In particular, agents retrieve less recent evidence even though recent evidence is more predictive of near-future activity. This is a useful signal for training research agents: supervision on final answers alone may miss failures in the search and evidence-selection policy.","technical_approach":"$2a","experiments":"$2b","reading_guide":"Skim time: 25–35 minutes. The key experimental results are the EWMA comparison and the finding that State carry-forward beats direct Forecast. The finetuning section can be read quickly; its main message is that RAP can provide a training signal, while the acquisition bias remains the harder mechanism-level issue.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读§实验和frozen-evidence replay：它提醒agent评测里强LLM可能输给干净统计基线，别只看搜索轨迹。","en":"Read the experiments and frozen-evidence replay: it is a useful warning that agentic search can lose to clean statistical baselines despite plausible traces."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yingqian Wu","Jingcong Liang","Siyuan Wang","Zhenfei Yin","Philip Torr","Junchi Yu","Zhongyu Wei"],"notable_authors":["Philip Torr"],"tags":["agent-evaluation","research-agents","temporal-benchmark","retrieval-bias","forecasting"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10092","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.10092","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.09528","title":"MotionBlind: Probing the Illusion of Motion Understanding in Video-LLMs","abstract":"Video large language models (Video-LLMs) are increasingly used as the perceptual front end of world models, a role that assumes they can read motion: how fast something moves, which way it travels, how hard it is pushed. We show they cannot. A Video-LLM can watch two clips of the same person in the same room, name every object in both, and still fail to say which clip moves faster. We introduce MotionBlind, a contrastive benchmark of self-recorded video for physically grounded motion(speed, magn","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-08T23:15:20Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_09528.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的问题很直接：Video-LLM 看起来会“理解视频”，但它到底有没有稳定读取运动信息的能力，尤其是速度、方向、位移幅度这类对 world model 和 embodied system 很关键的物理运动属性。作者认为现有视频问答 benchmark 往往高估了模型的时序理解，因为很多题目可以靠单帧静态线索、场景共现、语言先验来猜对，而不需要真正读出 motion。MotionBlind 的核心任务，就是把静态内容控制住，只让两段视频在运动属性上有差别，再用严格的对比式评测去测模型是否真的看懂了运动。","importance":"这不是预训练方法论文，但对多模态基座研究有明确价值，尤其是 native video pretrain、frame sampling、视频 tokenizer 和 world-model 接口设计。它提供的启发有两层。第一，很多 Video-LLM 的“视频能力”可能主要来自静态视觉识别和语言补全，而不是对时间变化的建模；如果这一点成立，那么把 Video-LLM 直接当作 world model 的感知前端会有明显风险。第二，作者系统测试了 frame 数量、采样策略、时间顺序打乱、无视频文本探针等因素，说明问题未必只是模型参数不够，而可能是训练目标、视频表示和时序归纳偏置都没有真正对准 motion understanding。对做视频预训练的人，这篇工作更像一个诊断工具：它告诉你该怀疑什么，而不是直接给出修复方案。","technical_approach":"方法上，核心贡献是 benchmark 和诊断协议，而不是新模型。MotionBlind 采用 contrastive minimal-pair 设计。每个 instance 由两段几乎相同的视频组成，场景、人物、背景、物体尽量保持一致，只改变一个运动变量，比如向左还是向右、快还是慢、位移更大还是更小。每个视频再配两个互补的 yes/no 问题，因此一个 instance 一共四个判断项。只有四项全对，instance 才算正确。这个 IAcc 指标很严格，能有效压制“靠猜中一半”带来的虚高分数。\n\n数据构造上，作者选择 self-recorded clips，而不是互联网视频。原因很明确：互联网视频很难把静态内容控制到足够一致，也难精确标注物理运动变量。自采视频虽然规模小，但能把 motion 作为唯一主要变化因素。另一个设计点是相对运动问题。作者尽量避免“快/慢”“重/轻”这类依赖场景语义的绝对概念，而是让问题比较同一场景中的两个运动参考，从而减少语言定义歧义。\n\n诊断实验设计得比较完整。作者从三个维度 probing。第一是 video necessity：给模型只看文本问题，不给视频，测它能否靠语言先验蒙对。第二是 temporal-order sensitivity：把帧顺序打乱或反转，若模型性能几乎不变，说明它没有真正利用时间顺序。第三是 frame selection：比较 uniform sampling、random sampling、一个 GRPO 训练的 learned selector（HORNet）以及 training-free 的 key-clip selector（Frame2Clip），并在 1 到 24 帧的不同预算下测试。这个设计很有信息量，因为它把“模型不懂 motion”进一步拆成几个可能来源：是单帧就够了，还是多帧也没用；是采样策略不对，还是模型即使拿到更好的帧也读不出运动。","experiments":"实验结论很清楚：当前 Video-LLM 在物理运动理解上表现很差，而且这种差不是普通视频 QA 分数能反映出来的。作者把 MotionBlind 与 TimeBlind、Video-MME 一起使用，形成一个对照。Video-MME 这类非对比式 benchmark 往往显示模型“总体视频能力不错”，但到了 MotionBlind 这种严格控制静态内容、只考 motion 差异的设置，性能明显塌陷。摘要和引言里提到，类似对比协议下，强模型在 TimeBlind 上 instance-level accuracy 也只有 48.2%，远低于人类 98.2%。MotionBlind 的目标就是把这种失败模式进一步收缩到 speed、direction、magnitude 这些物理变量上。\n\n更关键的是诊断结果。无视频文本探针可以检验题目是否被语言先验泄漏；若文本-only 也能拿到不低分数，说明 benchmark 设计有漏洞。作者的设置显然是想把这条路堵住。打乱帧序和反转帧序若只带来有限退化，说明模型主要在读静态帧，而不是时序关系。增加帧数和改进采样策略若提升有限，则进一步说明问题不只是“没看到关键帧”，而是模型缺少把帧间差异编码成运动表征的能力。全文片段没有给出 MotionBlind 上各模型的完整数值表，但作者的叙述已经足够明确：即便是前沿 Video-LLM，在这种严格 instance-level 指标下也远未接近可靠运动理解；而更复杂的 frame selector 也不能从根上补齐这个缺口。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点看 benchmark 设计和 probing 逻辑。先读 Introduction，理解作者为什么认为传统视频 QA 高估了时序能力。然后看 MotionBlind 的数据构造原则：self-recorded、minimal pair、relative motion、2×2 对比问答、IAcc 严格计分。这里是整篇最有价值的部分，因为它决定了 benchmark 到底在测什么。\n\n实验部分建议按 probing 维度读，而不是按模型列表读。先看 no-video probe，再看 shuffled/reversed frames，最后看 frame selection 和 frame The authors argue that many existing video-QA benchmarks overestimate temporal understanding because questions can often be answered from a representative frame, object co-occurrence, or language priors, without reading motion itself. DCP translates claims about research outcomes into executable recovery and feedback tests. It consists of three Gates: Gate 1 validates useful improvement on a sealed evaluation; Gate 2 provides matched agents with initial information and observed web content while withholding the target research history, requiring zero observed recoveries to reach the numerical target and providing a finite-sample bound on recovery probability; Gate 3 (optional) measures the average effect of truthful feedback relative to a specified neutral policy. PDDL problem（给定 domain 文件）时，常用的“能否解析/能否被 planner 求解”会高估语义忠实度：生成的 problem 可能可解但表达了错误的初始状态、目标、对象结构或优化指标。论文要解决的是：如何在更贴近部署的端到端流程里，对 NL-to-PDDL problem generation 做 grounded 的评估，并用可操作的反馈信号驱动迭代修复。","importance":"对预训练本身是间接相关：它不是新的预训练目标或 scaling 结论，而是一个典型的“形式化约束 + LLM 生成 + 反馈修复”的系统化评测与流程设计。对基座研究的启发点在两处：\n1) 评测信号质量：仅靠表面可行性（parse/solve）会产生虚高指标，类似于很多 LM 任务里‘能跑通’但语义错位的情况。\n2) 训练/对齐数据构造：这种细粒度错误分解（对象/谓词/goal/metric/numeric fluent）天然适合做合成数据与 verifier 信号，未来可反哺到 mid-train 或 RL-from-feedback 的 reward 设计。","technical_approach":"提出一个统一的生成-检查-反馈-修复循环（problem 级别，不是 domain 级别）：\n- 输入：NL 问题描述 + 给定的 PDDL domain 文件。\n- 生成：LLM 直接生成完整 problem.pddl（objects、init、goal，以及 PDDL 2.1 下的 metric 与 numeric 初始化）。\n- Operational checks（可上线意义的验收）：\n  1) PDDL 解析；\n  2) domain-conformance checker：检查 problem 是否只使用 domain 定义的谓词/类型/结构；\n  3) planner 求解；\n  4) 用 VAL 做计划验证（确保 plan 真能在该 problem 上执行并达成目标）；\n  5) 额外的 NL critic：结合 domain、生成的 problem、原 NL 描述做语义层面的批评与定位。\n- Iterative repair：把上述检查器产生的结构化反馈喂回 LLM，迭代修改 problem，直到 operational acceptance。\n- 评测上区分两类指标：\n  1) operational acceptance（能解析、符合 domain、可规划、可验证）；\n  2) benchmark-reference reconstruction（对齐到数据集里人工策划的 reference problem），并实证两者差距。\n- 覆盖 PDDL 2.1：针对 temporal / numeric fluent 的差异做 metric-sensitive checking 与 planner 选择。","experiments":"从引言可确认的实验结论与设置（具体数值在当前片段中未出现）：\n- 数据集/基准：Planetarium、AutoPlanBench，以及作者整理的 PDDL 2.1（含 temporal 与 numeric）设置。\n- 发现：few-shot prompting 与 iterative repair 在多个设置下提升 operational performance；但要重建 curated reference 仍然困难，PDDL 2.1 尤其难。\n- 论文还专门量化“operational acceptance 与 reference reconstruction 的 gap”，并测试 repair 流程向 PDDL 2.1 的迁移效果。\n若关心可复用性，建议重点看：domain-conformance checker 的规则覆盖面、VAL 失败信息如何被转成可用的修复提示、以及 critic 在错误定位中的增益是否可被消融验证。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 分钟）：\n- 先看 轮数的边际收益、以及失败类型统计（init/goal/object/metric/numeric）。\n- 如果只想拿走工程结论：关注“哪些检查最有用、哪些反馈最容易让 LLM 修对”，这决定了系统复杂度与收益比。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In NL-to-PDDL problem generation (given a domain file), standard success criteria—parsing and planner solvability—can dramatically overestimate semantic faithfulness. A problem instance may be valid and solvable while encoding the wrong initial state, goals, objects, or optimization metric. The paper builds a deployment-oriented pipeline to evaluate generation more groundedly and to iteratively repair errors using structured feedback.","importance":"Indirectly relevant to pretraining: it does not propose a new pretraining objective or scaling result, but it is a clean case study of how weak acceptance signals inflate performance and how to replace them with tighter, decomposed verification signals. The error decomposition and verifier outputs are also a plausible substrate for synthetic data generation or reward design (mid-training / RL-from-feedback) in domains where formal constraints exist.","technical_approach":"An end-to-end generate–check–feedback–repair loop for PDDL problem instances:\n- Input: natural-language problem description + a given PDDL domain.\n- Generation: an LLM produces a full problem.pddl (objects, init, goal; plus metrics and numeric fluents for PDDL 2.1).\n- Operational evaluation stack:\n  1) PDDL parsing;\n  2) domain-conformance checking (types/predicates/vocabulary consistency with the domain);\n  3) planning;\n  4) plan validation via VAL;\n  5) an LLM critic that cross-references the NL description, the domain, and the generated problem to produce fine-grained repair hints.\n- Iterative repair: feed structured feedback back into the LLM to revise the problem until it passes operational acceptance.\n- Evaluation explicitly separates operational acceptance from reference reconstruction against curated benchmark instances, and studies the gap.\n- Extends to PDDL 2.1 with temporal/numeric constructs via metric-sensitive checks and planner selection.","experiments":"From the provided excerpt (without numeric tables):\n- Benchmarks include Planetarium, AutoPlanBench, and curated PDDL 2.1 settings.\n- Few-shot prompting paper measures how operational acceptance diverges from stricter reference-based reconstruction and tests transfer of the repair protocol to PDDL 2.1.\nKey implementation details to verify in the full control","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-09T17:59:32Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2609_10540.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“视频生成更像”，而是“世界模型怎样真正变成可交互、可编程、可长期维护状态的世界引擎”。现有视频 world model 大多把历史画面和动作直接映射到下一段观测，短期交互可以成立，但一旦要求显式操控单个实体、维护屏外对象与非视觉属性、或者让用户写出可持续生效的规则，纯观测驱动的生成范式就不够用了。论文把问题拆成两部分：一部分是可执行的世界状态演化，另一部分是基于状态的视觉生成；关键难点在于两者之间需要一种既紧凑、可程序编辑，又足以约束视频生成的中间表示。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接启发不在于语言模型本身，而在于“生成模型是否应显式建模持久状态”这个更大的建模问题。它提出了一种把可执行符号状态与生成式渲染解耦的路线，接近把 world model 从纯序列预测器改造成“状态机 + 生成器”的组合系统。对多模态预训练研究，这有三层意义。第一，说明统一生成并不一定要把所有约束都压进隐状态里，显式状态接口可以降低长期一致性负担。第二，给出了一个较务实的中间表示选择：不是重建完整 3D scene graph 或 mesh，而是用 state-augmented 3D OBB 作为可编辑控制面。第三，它把数据问题讲得比较清楚：训练时可从视频反推结构，推理时却要从用户意图正向构造结构，这两种分布并不一致，因此中间表示必须控制复杂度。对做 native multimodal pretrain、video LM、AR+world-state 混合建模的人，这篇文章有方法论价值。","technical_approach":"方法核心是三段式系统。第一段是 coding agent。给定单张参考图和自然语言描述，VLM-based agent 负责初始化世界：识别实体、生成初始布局、补充属性与状态，并把交互规则、触发条件、任务目标写成可执行程序。第二段是 state executor。它不再依赖视频模型隐式记忆世界，而是维护一个显式全局状态；玩家动作和事件进入后，executor 按程序更新实体状态，包括屏外实体和非视觉字段。第三段是 generative renderer。它不直接接收自然语言规则，而是接收由 state compiler 生成的像素对齐时空控制信号，再合成视频观测。\n\n真正的设计重点在中间表示。作者比较了文本、2D box/mask、详细 3D scene、G-buffer 等方案后，选择 state-augmented 3D oriented bounding box。每个实体用共享世界坐标系下的 OBB 表示位置、尺寸、朝向，再附加 persistent identity、语义类别、动态状态、外观信息。这个选择的理由很明确：比文本更有几何约束，比 2D 控制更视角无关，比 mesh/skeleton 更轻，不要求推理时显式求解细粒度姿态和复杂几何。换句话说，它把“哪些约束必须显式给出”与“哪些细节交给生成器补全”做了切分。\n\nstate compiler 是另一个关键部件。它把 evolving OBB state 沿目标相机轨迹投影并 rasterize 成 pixel-aligned spatiotemporal controls。这样做不是直接让模型从结构 token 到图像，而是先用确定性编译器解决相机投影和实体到像素的对应关系，把 identity、semantic、orientation、motion、state change 这些强约束显式写进控制图，再让视频模型负责细几何、纹理、动作细节和次级物理效果。这个接口设计明显是在减少生成模型需要“自己学会”的几何对齐负担。\n\n训练侧还配了一个数据整理管线，用于从视频中恢复这种接口所需的空间和语义监督。虽然给出的正文片段没有展开全部实现细节，但从引言可知，作者把它当成可扩展训练数据的必要条件：如果没有从视频中抽取实体级 3D/语义轨迹的管线，这套表示就很难规模化训练。","experiments":"从全文可见的重点实验有两类。第一类是可控一致性评测，作者提出 CombatStateBench，专门测生成视频是否与 engine 维护的世界状态一致。指标聚焦在 combat 场景里最容易出错、也最能暴露状态失配的问题：可见存活角色数量是否正确，以及死亡状态是否被视觉上真实呈现。这个 benchmark 的价值在于它不只看视频质量，而是看“状态执行结果有没有被渲染器忠实实现”。第二类应是系统级对比与消融：比较有无显式 state、不同控制表示、以及编译控制对长期一致性和实体级控制的影响。引言已经明确声称该框架支持 entity-level control、persistent state management 和 programmable rules，并通过 benchmark 验证状态-视频一致性。\n\n不过，当前提供的全文片段没有包含结果表、具体数值、模型参数量、训练数据规模、以及各项提升幅度，因此无法负责任地填写精确数字。若按阅读优先级判断，实验部分更应该关注的是误差类型而不是单一分数：例如哪些失败来自状态构建错误，哪些来自 renderer 未能遵守控制，哪些来自长时交互中的累计漂移。这些比平均指标更能说明方法边界。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，但建议带着“中间表示为什么这样选”去看。优先看 Introduction 和 Figure 2，那里把文本、2D 控制、详细 3D 结构、G-buffer 这些候选接口的取舍讲得比较透。第二个重点是 state-augmented 3D OBB 与 deterministic state compiler 的定义，这部分决定了系统到底是在做真正的状态解耦，还是只是给视频模型加条件。第三个重点是 CombatStateBench 的设计，因为它体现了作者如何把“世界状态一致性”从主观观感里剥离出来，变成可测目标。\n\n如果时间有限，30-40 分钟足够抓住主线：问题设定、表示选择、编译接口、benchmark。若要深挖，建议重点看数据整理管线和失败案例。前者关系到这条路线能否规模化，后者关系到显式状态与生成渲染之间的真实耦合程度。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not mainly about making video generation more realistic. It asks a harder systems question: how to turn a video world model into an interactive world engine with persistent state, entity-level control, and programmable rules. Existing video world models usually map visual history and actions to future observations. That works for short-horizon interaction, but it breaks when the system must track off-screen entities, maintain non-visual attributes such as inventory or task progress, or enforce executable rules over time. The paper separates world-state evolution from observation generation, and the central technical question becomes the interface between the two: what representation is compact enough to be editable by programs, yet structured enough to guide rendering.","importance":"For pretraining researchers, the value is methodological rather than directly tied to text LMs. The paper argues that long-horizon multimodal generation may need explicit state rather than relying entirely on latent memory inside a sequence model. That matters for world models, native multimodal pretraining, and unified generative systems. A second contribution is representational: instead of going all the way to meshes, skeletons, or dense 3D scene descriptions, it uses state-augmented 3D OBBs as a controllable middle layer. A third contribution is about train-test mismatch. During training, structure is extracted from observed videos; during inference, structure must be constructed from desired state transitions. The paper makes the case that the intermediate representation should be chosen with that mismatch in mind. This is useful for anyone working on explicit memory, structured control, or hybrid symbolic-generative modeling.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"$30","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper unless your work is close to world models or structured multimodal pretraining. Existing work on asynchronous LLM training corrects the actor and leaves this bias unaddressed, while the off-policy value correction of classical RL does not carry over to long-horizon agentic tasks, since a short correction horizon leaves the regression target free of the reward and a long one lets the product of","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-09T06:32:30Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_09783.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"异步 RL 扩展 LLM 训练吞吐时，rollout policy μ 与正在更新的 target policy π 存在版本滞后。已有方法主要修正 actor 的 off-policy 问题，但 critic 的回归目标仍来自旧策略采样轨迹，因而会学到 stale behavior policy 的 value，而不是当前策略需要的 value。这个偏差在长 horizon、终局奖励、agentic task 中会累积到早期 token，直接污染 advantage。","importance":"贡献点在 critic-side correction。长链路 LLM RL 训练已经越来越依赖异步 rollout，否则同步训练会被最慢样本拖住。若只修 actor，不修 value target，PPO 类方法的 baseline 会系统性偏向旧策略，优势估计会把策略进步错误地抵消掉。BRACE 提供了一个面向 LLM 终局奖励结构的 value correction 方案。它不是简单搬用 V-trace，而是处理了 V-trace 在 token-level、长序列、terminal reward 场景下的两个失败模式：短窗口没有 reward 信号，长窗口 importance-ratio 乘积随长度漂移。","technical_approach":"工作把生成过程写成 token-level MDP：状态是 prompt 加已生成前缀，转移由追加 token 决定，reward 只在序列结束时由 verifier 给出。普通 GAE/TD 目标在异步数据上回归时，条件期望对应 μ 的 Bellman operator；反复拟合后 critic 收敛到 V^μ，而 actor 更新需要的是 V^π。这个差距不是一个 batch 常数，位置越靠前越明显，因为剩余 suffix 全由 stale policy 生成，mismatch 会沿剩余长度累积。\n\nBRACE 的设计是“短程做策略校正，长程保留奖励传递”。具体做法包括两部分。第一，k-capped correction horizon：从每个位置开始，只对后续 k 个 policy tokens 使用 importance correction，避免把 π/μ 的乘积一路乘到终点。第二，anchored Monte-Carlo tail：超过 k 的后缀不继续滚动乘积，而用一个常数权重锚住 Monte-Carlo tail，把终局 reward 传回每个位置。这样，近处 token 的 off-policy residual 被校正，远端 reward 又不会因为短 TD 窗口而消失。与 V-trace 相比，BRACE 的关键区别是把“策略分布校正”和“终局奖励覆盖”拆开；V-trace 若窗口短，target 中没有终局 residual，早期状态看不到 reward；若窗口长，importance ratio product 会随长序列变得不稳定。","experiments":"实验覆盖 4 个 long-horizon agentic tasks、16 个指标。BRACE 在 13/16 个指标上取得最高结果；在 BrowseComp-Plus 上，相比最强 baseline 的 mean@1 提升 2.4%。吞吐方面，异步训练达到同步执行 2.46 倍 step rate。稳定性方面，在 version gap 达到 50 的条件下训练仍保持稳定。消融信息的核心信号是：只做 actor-side stale correction 不足以解决长 horizon 中的 advantage 偏差；只用传统 V-trace 会在 reward 覆盖和 ratio 稳定性之间两难；k-capped correction 加 anchored MC tail 才能同时保留终局奖励信号并限制 off-policy 方差。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 Motivation 中对 critic bias 的推导，特别是普通 value regression 为什么会收敛到 V^μ，以及 bias 为什么在 early positions 更大。随后看 V-trace 失败分析：短 horizon target 不含 terminal reward，长 horizon ratio product 不稳定。方法部分需要重点看 BRACE target 的构造、k 的作用、tail anchor 的权重定义，以及它如何进入 PPO/GAE 训练管线。实验部分建议重点看四类任务的主表、BrowseComp-Plus 的 mean@1、吞吐与 version gap 稳定性曲线，以及 k、anchor、actor correction 的消融。值得深挖的是该方法和异步 RL 系统的接口：rollout 队列版本差、critic 更新频率、ratio clipping、advantage normalization 之间会相互影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Asynchronous RL for LLMs improves utilization by decoupling rollout generation from optimization, but it creates policy lag. Existing LLM RL methods mostly correct the actor objective, while the critic is still trained on trajectories sampled by a stale behavior policy. As a result, the critic learns the value of the behavior policy μ rather than the current target policy π, and the resulting advantage estimates become biased, especially at early tokens in long-horizon tasks.","importance":"The main contribution is a critic-side correction tailored to long-horizon LLM RL with terminal rewards. This is a practical pretrain/posttrain interface issue: large-scale reasoning and agent training increasingly rely on asynchronous rollout systems, and critic bias can become a training-signal quality bottleneck. BRACE identifies why a classical off-policy value correction such as V-trace does not directly fit LLM trajectories, then proposes a target that preserves reward propagation while bounding importance-weight instability.","technical_approach":"$31","experiments":"The evaluation covers four long-horizon agentic tasks and 16 metrics. BRACE leads on 13 of the 16 metrics. On BrowseComp-Plus, it improves mean@1 by 2.4% over the strongest baseline. The asynchronous setup runs at 2.46 times the step rate of synchronous execution, and training remains stable up to a version gap of 50. The ablations indicate that actor-side stale-policy correction alone does not remove the value-target bias, and that a V-trace-style correction faces a reward-coverage versus ratio-stability tradeoff. The combination of capped local correction and an anchored Monte Carlo tail is the central empirical driver.","reading_guide":"Read carefully. It needs no gradient-based training and no optimization, only a few hundred contrastive prompts, which makes it the canonical white-box attack on open-weight alignment. mo","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-09T06:48:08Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_09793.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CR","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个很具体但很现实的问题：在前沿规模、MoE 架构、量化权重和多流残差同时存在的情况下，经典的 refusal-direction ablation 还能不能把安全拒答行为从模型里拆掉。如果能，攻击真正作用在网络的哪里；如果不能，失效边界在哪里。作者不是在做 jailbreak benchmark，而是在做白盒安全机制分析：对一个 320B MoE 开源模型，单方向攻击是否仍成立，以及过去在 dense 模型上流传的实现 recipe 在 residual、量化这些前沿架构细节，会改变机制解释和参数编辑的可达性，这对以后讨论“预训练表示 vs 对齐附加层”很重要。贡献量级上，这不是提出新攻击范式，而是把已有 directional ablation 从 70B 级 dense 模型推进到 320B 级 frontier MoE，并把失败模式、可编辑模块边界和残余拒答类别讲清楚。","technical_approach":"方法核心仍是 directional ablation，但作者把它改造成适配 MoE + 多流残差 + FP8 量化的版本。基本思路是先用少量 contrastive prompts 提取 refusal direction，再把所有会向 residual stream 写入该方向的权重做正交投影，从而移除拒答行为。难点有三个。第一，MoE 里“谁在写 residual”不再只是 attention 和 dense MLP，还包括 routed experts 与 shared projections。作者发现如果沿用 dense 模型时代按 module name 遍历的 recipe，很多 fused expert 参数根本不会被命中，代码会显示 edit 成功，但实际几乎没改到关键位置，这就是 silent failure。第二，GLM-5.3-Flash 采用 four-wide hyper-connection residual，多条并行流在层间混合。作者分析后指出，这种结构并不阻碍方向消融，因为 mixing weights 是标量；只要某个方向在每条 stream 上都被压成零，后续线性混合仍然是零。因此关键不是在 layer output 上做 hook，而是直接编辑 residual-writing weights，这样覆盖更彻底。第三，作者检验“单方向”假设在 frontier scale 是否还成立。他们不仅做 rank-1 方向消融，还尝试更高秩子空间，并用随机正交方向作为对照，确认效果不是“删掉任意一个方向都会让模型更顺从”。全文最重要的技术发现有两个：一是 refusal 的可编辑部分高度分布式，attention、dense/shared projections、routed experts 单独改都不够，联合编辑才出现大部分效果；二是即便如此，仍有一部分 refusal residue 无法被低秩子空间去掉，而且集中在少数内容类别上，说明安全行为并不完全压缩成一个简单线性方向。","experiments":"实验对象是 GLM-5.3-Flash：320B 参数、288 routed experts、四路 hyper-connection residual、block-FP8 权重。作者把 edit effect 分解到不同 writer group 的所有子集上，结果显示强非加性。单独编辑 attention、dense/shared projections、routed experts，分别只移除 0.039、0.016、0.148 的 refusal；三者一起编辑时可移除 0.776。也就是说，约四分之三的效果不属于任何单个模块，而是只在联合编辑时出现。这一点很关键，因为它直接否定了“拒答主要写在某一类层里”的简单图景。更具体地，传统按 module-name matching 的实现通常只会碰到 attention 和 dense projections，这一子集只能移除 0.066 / 0.776，可见在 MoE 上几乎等于没打到要害，但表面上又不会报错。作者还比较了两种实施方式：hook layer boundary 与直接编辑 residual-writing weights，配对比较显示后者更彻底。为了证明攻击不是随便删个方向都有效，他们用与 refusal direction 正交的随机方向做消融，结果几乎不改变 refusal。泛化评测上，在 7 个 harmful benchmarks 上，拒答率下降 41 到 89 个百分点，同时未检测到 capability 下降。这个区间本身也揭示了边界：下降较少的 benchmark 恰好富含那些“顽固类别”。作者进一步尝试针对这些类别拟合 rank up to 12 的子空间，仍然存在残余拒答，说明至少在这个模型上，安全对齐并不完全是单方向、甚至也不完全是低秩线性子空间可去除的。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但要抓住两个层面：工程实现和机制结论。先看 Introduction，作者把 dense→MoE、单流→多流、FP16/FP32→FP8 这三处前提变化讲得很清楚。然后重点看方法部分里“哪些参数算 residual writers”，以及为什么 name-matching recipe 会漏掉 fused experts。接着看 writer-group subset decomposition 的结果，这是全文信息量最大的部分；如果只看一个表，就看这个。再看 hyper-connection residual 的分析，理解为什么多流结构没有从原理上阻断方向消融。最后看 resistant categories 和 higher-rank subspace 的实验，这部分决定了作者的结论不是“单方向攻击在大模型上完全成立”，而是“它能拆掉很大一部分拒答，但还有稳定残余”。预计 25–40 分钟可以读完主线。若要深挖，值得继续想三件事：这些残余类别是否对应更深层的 reward-model imprint；MoE 中 refusal 信号是写在 expert 内部表征还是 attention/expert 协同里；以及这种低秩可编辑性是否会随着更强 post-train 或更长 RL 过程而下降。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a concrete white-box security question: does classic refusal-direction ablation still work once the target model is a frontier-scale MoE with quantized weights and a multi-stream residual topology? If it works, where in the network does the edit actually need to land; if it fails, where is the boundary? This is not mainly a jailbreak benchmark paper. It is a mechanistic study of whether a 320B open-weight MoE still exposes refusal as a removable low-dimensional behavior, and why recipes that worked on dense models can fail silently on modern architectures.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is indirect but real. The paper probes the geometry of alignment in parameter space and how that geometry changes with architecture. For pretraining and model-building work, there are two useful implications. First, if post-training safety behavior lives in a very low-dimensional subspace, then alignment may be more superficial and easier to strip than many deployment assumptions suggest. Second, MoE sparsity, hyper-connection residuals, and quantization all affect what mechanistic edits can reach, which matters when reasoning about the boundary between pretrained representations and post-trained behavioral overlays. The contribution is not a new attack family; it is an extension and stress test of directional ablation from dense models up to a 320B frontier MoE, with careful analysis of silent failure modes, editable writer groups, and the categories of refusal that remain resistant.","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"$33","reading_guide":"$34","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会改变你对“对齐已融入大模型内部”的乐观看法；重点看 refusal direction 在 MoE writer 间如何分散，防御思路得从表示层改起。","en":"This should weaken any optimism that alignment is deeply baked in; focus on how the refusal direction spreads across MoE writers, because defenses likely need representation-level changes."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yi Shi","Tanyu Chen","Kai Shen"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["alignment","MoE","refusal-direction","white-box-attack","safety"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09793","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.09793","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.09707","title":"Which Tokens Should SFT Actually Learn? A Token-Trimming Perspective on Mathematical Reasoning","abstract":"Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) applies a uniform cross-entropy loss to all target tokens, even though different tokens provide unequal learning signals for mathematical reasoning. This uniform treatment can over-sharpen already mastered tokens while amplifying learning pressure on uncertain, low-confidence tokens, leading to suboptimal training dynamics. It significantly mitigates representation collapse and improves reasoning efficiency and accuracy without adding inference cost, offering a promising training paradigm for latent reasoning beyond the next-token prediction.","technical_approach":"The proposed PMPS (Prototype-Mediated Process Supervision) framework features: 1) Learnable reasoning prototypes as semantic anchors to project latent and explicit CoT embeddings into a shared space. 2) Sinkhorn-Knopp-based many-to-many soft alignment between unequal-length representations, coupled with a bidirectional cross-prediction loss to enforce differentiated semantics. 3) A Progressive Sequential Alignment (PSA) module that uses a Gaussian positional prior for initial sequential guidance, gradually relaxed via cosine annealing for adaptive matching.","experiments":"On GSM8K-Aug using GPT-2, PMPS reduces reasoning length by over 50% compared to explicit CoT while improving accuracy by 3.96%. 接口的启发在于：跨模型蒸馏的问题不只是 tokenizer 对齐，也不只是教师更强就更好。不同模型家族的 likelihood 标尺和策略偏置会形成很大的固定偏移，这个偏移可能主导 token-level 更新方向，掩盖真正来自教师能力提升的信号。这个观察对用闭源强模型、异构开源模型做蒸馏或 RL-style token supervision 决定。CompassOPD 的做法是去掉跨家族 offset，只迁移教师家族内部随能力提升而产生的 likelihood 变化。学生侧则用冻结的初始学生策略作为 anchor，衡量当前学生相对初始化的位移，避免直接匹配外部教师的绝对 likelihood。这样教师侧和学生侧的比较都尽量留在各自家族内部，核心目标从“模仿外部教师概率”改成“沿着教师能力提升对应的方向更新”。","experiments":"实验覆盖三个学生模型家族和多个教师家族，在相同学生初始化下比较标准 cross-family OPD 与 CompassOPD。主要现象包括：同家族 OPD 能持续提升，跨家族 OPD 很快 plateau；能力差距很大的外部教师蒸馏后学生性能接近；同一教师家族内最强模型和最小模型在相同学生轨迹上，对约 70% 的 aligned textual units 给出相同更新方向。CompassOPD 在平均 reasoning accuracy 上相对标准跨家族 OPD 最高提升 5.50 个点。消融显示，把被移除的 cross-family offset 加回去会降低性能；固定教师家族参考模型时，教师规模增大能更稳定转化为学生性能提升。对 MoE 教师，论文还通过减少 expert activation 从教师 checkpoint 构造低能力 reference，减少对单独发布弱参考模型的依赖，并仍能保持相对 OPD 的增益。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中的 Figure MoE reference 构造。若做异构蒸馏或 token-level RL supervision，这篇的诊断部分比最终算法更值得细看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks why on-policy distillation breaks down when the teacher and student come from different model families. Standard OPD uses teacher token likelihoods on student-sampled trajectories. It works well within a family, but in cross-family settings the gains saturate quickly even after tokenizer or text-space alignment, and stronger external teachers do not reliably produce better students.","importance":"The useful lesson is that cross-family distillation is not solved by aligning tokenizers, and teacher strength is not sufficient by itself. Absolute likelihoods carry family-specific offsets induced by tokenizer choices, pretraining recipes, and policy geometry. These offsets can dominate token-level update directions and drown out the part of the signal that reflects the teacher’s capability improvement. This matters for distilling from heterogeneous open or closed models, especially when the supervision is dense and likelihood-based.","technical_approach":"The authors decompose the cross-family OPD signal into two terms: a cross-family offset between a weak reference model from the teacher family and the student, and a within-family likelihood shift from that weak reference to the strong teacher. Standard OPD transfers both terms together. CompassOPD removes the offset and transfers only the within-family shift associated with teacher improvement. On the student side, a frozen copy of the initial student policy is used as an anchor, so the update is modulated by how far the current student has moved from its initialization. The design avoids matching absolute probabilities from an external family and instead uses relative changes measured inside each model family.","experiments":"The experiments span three student families and multiple teacher families under matched student initializations. The paper reports three diagnostic findings: same-family OPD keeps improving while cross-family OPD plateaus; external teachers with substantially different capabilities lead to similar distilled students; and, on the same student trajectories, the strongest and smallest models in a teacher family agree on the update direction for about 70% of aligned textual units. CompassOPD improves average reasoning accuracy over standard cross-family OPD by up to 5.50 points. Ablations show that restoring the removed cross-family offset hurts performance. With a fixed teacher-family reference, larger teachers translate into progressively better students. For MoE teachers, the authors also construct a low-capability reference by reducing expert activation from the teacher checkpoint itself, avoiding the need for a separately released weak reference model.","reading_guide":"A 30-minute skim is enough for most readers. Start with Figure 1, since it motivates the failure mode: cross-family plateauing, weak dependence on teacher capability, and high agreement between strong and weak teacher update directions. Then read the signal decomposition and the CompassOPD update construction. In the experiments, focus on the matched-initialization comparisons, the offset restoration ablation, and the MoE reference construction. Its value is in connecting safety behavior to temporal adaptation and parameter-space write paths, rather than treating steering as a black-box defense.","technical_approach":"The authors analyze the optimization trajectory of Preventative Steering, where undesirable-trait persona vectors are injected during fine-tuning and removed at evaluation. Intervention Delta Preservation and IDP Continuation test whether preserving or reinjecting the weight offset is enough. We implement the specified protocol and evaluate 200 independen","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-09T15:13:42Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_10299.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文复现并测量 MAIS-O43 提出的合成 sparse autoencoder 相图实验，检验在 nested feature co-occurrence 下，SAE 何时恢复真实字典、何时发生 feature merge 或 split。实际结果是：在已测网格中没有恢复，也没有 merge，而是稳定进入一种 diffuse phase。","importance":"它对基座模型机制分析的价值在于提醒：SAE 的训练结果不一定对应稀疏编码理论里预期的全局最优结构。即使重建误差接近完美，decoder atoms 也可能离真实特征很远。对用 SAE 解释 LLM activations 的工作，这意味着不能只看 reconstruction 和 sparsity，还需要检查 learned atoms 是否真的对应可识别的生成特征。论文也指出，关于 feature absorption 的理论预测可能描述的是目标函数的某些解，而不是实际优化器容易到达的解。","technical_approach":"论文严格实现 MAIS-O43 协议。合成数据在 n=64 维空间中由 m=256 个真实特征生成；部分 parent-child 特征对被设置为 nested support，即 child 不会在 parent 未激活时出现。实验扫描 nesting fraction γ、稀疏惩罚 λ 和字典大小 M。SAE 使用 ReLU encoder、unit-norm decoder columns、无 output bias，并用 full-batch Adam 训练 2×10^5 步。恢复、merge、split 都用预定义几何判据和 bipartite matching 判定。一个重要细节是，γ 的变化同时改变了依赖关系和 child 的边际频率，因此 γ 轴上的效果混合了 nesting 与 rarity 两个因素。","experiments":"正式协议测了 10 个 canonical grid cells，共 200 个独立初始化：包括 M=256 下 γ=0.5 的 λ sweep、λ=10^-2 下多个 γ 值，以及一个 M=512、γ=0.5、λ=10^-3 的 cell。每个 γ 使用 2^18 个固定训练样本和 2^16 个评估样本。结果是 200 次训练中 full-dictionary recovery 为 0，merge 为 0；模型通常能达到近乎完美的 reconstruction，但 learned atoms 与真实特征匹配比例低，形成可复现的 diffuse phase。鲁棒性实验显示，训练步数翻倍不改变结论；off-protocol 的 minibatch Adam 以约 64 倍速度达到相同 plateau，并覆盖全部 165 个 grid cells，也没有出现 recovery 或 merge。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20 到 30 分钟。重点看协议定义里的数据生成过程、nested support 如何改变 child frequency，以及 recovery/merge/split 的判定标准。结果部分重点看 diffuse phase 的证据：重建很好但 atoms 不对齐真实特征。讨论部分值得看训练动态与全局最优之间的差别，以及作者对 matched-frequency control 的提醒。若关注 SAE 在 LLM 解释中的可靠性，可以重点读 Section 5 到 Section 7。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper measures the synthetic SAE phase-diagram experiment proposed by MAIS-O43. The intended question is when a sparse autoencoder trained on nested feature co-occurrences recovers the true dictionary, merges correlated features, or splits them. In the measured cells, none of these expected outcomes appears: every run enters a stable diffuse phase.","importance":"The result is relevant to mechanistic interpretability rather than pretraining recipes directly. It shows that near-perfect reconstruction is not enough evidence that SAE decoder atoms have recovered the underlying features. Trained SAEs may converge to solutions that differ from the global sparse-coding structures used in theoretical discussions of feature absorption. This is a useful caution for interpreting LLM activations with SAEs: optimization dynamics and identifiability need to be checked, not assumed.","technical_approach":"The authors implement the MAIS-O43 protocol with a synthetic dictionary in 64 dimensions and 256 true features. Consecutive feature pairs can be marked as parent-child nested pairs, where a child feature never activates unless its parent is active. The grid varies nesting fraction γ, sparsity penalty λ, and learned dictionary size M. The SAE has a ReLU encoder, unit-norm decoder columns, no output bias, and is trained with full-batch Adam for 200,000 updates. Recovery is decided by exact bipartite matching against the true dictionary; merge and split events are detected by predefined geometric criteria. A key protocol caveat is that increasing γ changes both dependence structure and child marginal frequency, so the nesting axis also introduces rarity effects.","experiments":"The formal measurements cover 10 of the 165 canonical grid cells, with 20 independent initializations per cell and 200 fits in total. The slices include an M=256 λ sweep at γ=0.5, a γ row at λ=10^-2, and one M=512 cell at γ=0.5 and λ=10^-3. Each γ uses 2^18 fixed training samples and 2^16 evaluation samples. The outcome is zero full-dictionary recoveries and zero merges. Reconstruction is almost perfect, but learned atoms remain poorly aligned with the true features, producing a reproducible diffuse phase. Robustness checks show that doubling the update budget does not change the result. A minibatch Adam companion run reaches the same plateau about 64 times faster and covers all 165 cells without a single recovery or merge.","reading_guide":"A 20- to 30-minute skim is sufficient. Read the protocol details for the generator, the nesting intervention, and the exact outcome classifiers. Then focus on the result section showing the diffuse phase: good reconstruction with poor feature recovery. Prior work treats this concentration as bias, but it does not distinguish stable model preferences from responses that depend on a particular prompt wording. We introduce a bias depth score that measures both how strongly a model prefers its top answer under direct prompting and whether that answer survives scenario reframing. Across 4,442 opinion prompts and four large language models, only ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-09T08:57:22Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_09901.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“模型会不会偏向某个答案”这个旧问题，而是把两类常被混在一起的现象拆开：一类是模型在不同表述、不同场景下都稳定回到同一个答案；另一类只是被某个问法诱导，在换个说法后就散掉。作者把前者定义为 Deep bias，后者定义为 Shallow bias。这个区分很关键，因为如果不把“稳定偏好”和“提示词脆弱性”分开，就很难判断偏差来自预训练、SFT 还是后续对齐，也很难判断它是否真的能被后处理消掉。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它给“偏差”提供了一个更接近训练来源的分解方式。论文显示，能跨重述保留下来的偏差，更常在 pretrained checkpoint 中已经存在，并且会穿过 SFT、DPO、RLVR 继续保留；而只在单一问法下出现的偏差，则更像后训练阶段不断累积的表层行为。第二，它提醒人们：如果只用单 prompt 重复采样来测 bias，很容易把 prompt artifact 误判成模型内部稳定偏好，从而高估某些 debias 方法的效果。第三，这个框架对数据研究也有直接启发：预训练数据塑造的是更深层的语义吸引子，而 SFT 更容易改变表述层的响应模式。对做 continual pretrain、data mixture、alignment 反哺 pretrain 的人，这个结论是有操作意义的。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者构造了一个两轴度量。第一轴是 direct rate（DR）：对原始问题在固定温度 0.6 下采样 30 次，统计最常出现答案的占比，用来测“单一问法下模型有多集中”。第二轴是 framed rate（FR）：围绕同一个底层问题写 30 个日常场景重述，每个场景采样一次，但统计的不是各场景自己的 top answer，而是原始 direct top answer 在这些重述里还能出现多少次。这样做的目的很明确：不是看重述后模型是否仍然集中，而是看它是否仍然执着于同一个答案。最终用 π = DR × FR 作为 bias depth score，只有在原始问法下高度集中、且跨场景仍能回到同一答案时，分数才高。\n\n数据构造也有讲究。作者建立了 4,442 个 opinion prompt families，每个 family 都锚定到一个真实的 Olmo-3-7B-SFT 训练样本，再围绕同一语义问题写出 matched reframings。因为任务是开放式生成，不是多选题，所以他们还需要做答案归一化，把表面不同但语义相同的回答合并，否则 FR 会被措辞差异污染。论文的核心不是提出复杂模型，而是把“偏差是否跨表述稳定”这个因果上更有意义的问题，变成一个可批量评测的 protocol。\n\n更重要的是，作者没有停留在测量层，而是把这个框架沿着完整训练链路跑了一遍：Pretrained → SFT → DPO → RLVR。这样就能看偏差深度在各阶段如何迁移。随后又做两类归因：一类是看 SFT 后的 top answer 是否与 pretrained 一致，用来判断 Deep bias 是否继承自预训练；另一类是在 SFT 训练集中搜索可匹配的具体样本，测试能否把某个偏差追溯到单条训练数据。最后再用 continued LoRA-SFT 和 GEPA 这类 debias 手段验证：Deep/Shallow 两类偏差在可移除性上是否真的不同。","experiments":"实验里最重要的结果有四组。第一，在四个 post-SFT 模型上，按 direct prompting 看，35.8% 到 77.7% 的 prompt families 都表现出明显偏向；但这些 direct biases 里，只有大约四分之一能在 scenario reframing 下保留下来。也就是说，单 prompt 测到的大部分“偏差”其实是浅层的。第二，在完整的 Olmo-3-7B 流水线中，Deep bias 大多在 pretrained 阶段就已经存在，而 Shallow bias 会在 SFT 以及后续每个 post-training 阶段继续增长。这一点直接支持“深层偏好更多来自预训练，浅层偏好更容易被后训练塑形”的判断。第三，能跨重述保留的偏差，更可能与 pretrained model 的 top answer 一致；这说明 Deep bias 往往不是 SFT 新造出来的，而是被保留甚至强化。与此同时，作者也发现，把偏差精确归因到某一条 SFT 训练样本，只对少数案例成立，说明大部分偏差不是单样本记忆，而更像分布层面的统计吸引。第四，去偏实验显示 Deep bias 更难处理：continued LoRA-SFT 只把 Deep bias 降低 4.5 个百分点，却能把 Shallow bias 降低 10.3 个百分点；GEPA 这类 prompt-based debiasing 对 Deep bias 也只降 1.6 个百分点，对 Shallow bias 则降 5.8 个百分点。这个差距和前面的来源分析是对得上的。\n\n从预训练视角看，最有信息量的不是某个绝对分数，而是这几个现象之间的闭环：预训练继承性更强的偏差，更跨表述稳定，也更难被后处理消掉。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但建议把几个关键部分看细一点。先看引言和 Sec.2，把 DR、FR、bias depth score 的定义吃透，尤其要注意 FR 统计的是“direct top answer 在重述中的复现率”，不是 framed top answer 的集中度，这个设计决定了它测的是 persistence 而不是重新收敛。然后看 Sec.3 的数据构造，重点关注 prompt family、scenario reframing 和答案归一化规则；如果这里做得不严，后面的 Deep/Shallow 区分就站不住。实验部分优先看 Sec.4.2 到 4.4：一是训练阶段迁移，二是 pretrained/SFT 归因，三是 debias 难度差异。读图表时，重点找“同一 bias 在不同 checkpoint 上是否保留”和“不同 debias 方法对两类 bias 的降幅差”。\n\n预计 25 到 35 分钟能抓住主线。如果做数据或对齐研究，值得进一步深挖两个点：一是作者如何处理开放式答案匹配；二是那些无法追溯到单条 SFT 样本的偏差，是否更接近预训练语料中的频率结构、模板共现或文化先验。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not asking the old question of whether LLMs show answer concentration. It asks a sharper one: when a model keeps choosing the same answer, is that a stable preference that survives semantic reframing, or merely a wording-sensitive artifact of one prompt form? The authors call the former Deep bias and the latter Shallow bias. This distinction matters because prompt-level concentration alone cannot tell whether a behavior is rooted in pretraining, introduced by post-training, or simply elicited by a brittle surface form.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is fairly concrete. First, it offers a decomposition of bias that aligns better with training provenance. Biases that survive reframing are much more likely to already exist in the pretrained checkpoint and to persist through SFT, DPO, and RLVR. Biases that disappear under reframing look more like post-training-shaped response habits. Second, it shows that single-prompt repeated sampling is an unreliable proxy for model-internal preference: it overcounts prompt artifacts as if they were stable biases. Third, it suggests a useful division of labor between stages: pretraining appears to set deeper semantic attractors, while SFT more readily reshapes surface-level response patterns. That is relevant for data mixture design, continual pretraining, and for understanding what alignment can and cannot overwrite.","technical_approach":"$35","experiments":"$36","reading_guide":"$37","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不改方法，但改测量口径：先别把所有 bias 都算在 prompt 上，读 abstract 和核心定义就足够决定你后续 debias 实验怎么分层。","en":"It does not change the method stack, but it changes the measurement lens: stop blaming all bias on prompting; the definition and main result are enough to reshape debiasing ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":["MBZUAI","University of Michigan","KAIST","University of Virginia","Auburn University","Affiliation:MBZUAI","Affiliation:University of Michigan","Affiliation:KAIST","Affiliation:University of Virginia","Affiliation:Auburn University"],"authors":["An Vo","Vy Tuong Dang","Khai-Nguyen Nguyen","Emilio Villa-Cueva","Thamar Solorio","Anh Totti Nguyen","Daeyoung Kim"],"notable_authors":["Thamar Solorio"],"tags":["bias","mechanistic-analysis","prompt-sensitivity","SFT","pretraining-effects"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09901","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.09901","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.09206","title":"MLLMs Hallucinate when Information Distribution Drifts in Synergy Heads","abstract":"Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often struggle with hallucinations, thus hindering their reliable practical applications. obviou","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-08T19:46:08Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_09395.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是在线 bounded ordered tool menu construction，并提出 state path 作为学习目标。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是核心论文，但对 post-train agent 和“模型如何利用外部动作空间”有现实启发。价值主要有两点。第一，它把工具检索从单点相关性问题改写成路径覆盖问题，这比常见的 tool retrieval 设定更接近真实执行。第二，它把菜单看成 execution prior，而不是简单候选集，这个视角对 agent 系统设计很有用：很多失败不是推理能力不够，而是 agent 在第一步就没看到一条可执行链。对做 tool-use、agent benchmark、或者研究 action space shaping 的人，这篇文章值得泛读；对纯 pretrain 研究员，更多是了解 post-train 侧的接口约束。","technical_approach":"方法名是 State-Path Tool Menu，分 encoder、retriever、reranker 三部分。问题设定里，工具库中每个工具都有文档、输入字段和输出字段；请求 q 暴露一个初始状态 s0(q)，其中包含执行前可见的字段、对象和约束。系统要在固定预算 K=32 下构造一个有序菜单，agent 在整个执行过程中只能调用这 32 个工具。\n\n作者提出 state path 作为隐藏目标：它是从初始可见状态出发，经由若干工具产生中间字段，最终到达目标结果的一条预执行假设路径。路径中的工具被分成四类角色：entry tool 可以直接从初始状态运行；bridge tool 负责生成后续所需字段；target tool 完成主要请求；terminal tool 负责提交或交付结果，有时与 target 重合。这个角色化定义很重要，因为它把“相关”拆成了“现在能不能执行”“会不会为后续创造条件”“是不是最终动作”三种不同信号。\n\nencoder 的目标是学到 relation-aware tool representation。正文片段明确提到它引入了三个方向性信号，用来表示某工具是否当前可执行、是否会产生后续需要的字段、以及与其他工具的先后依赖关系。也就是说，它不是只编码工具描述文本，而是把 request state、tool schema、训练轨迹中的依赖关系一起编码进去。retriever 再利用这些表示，从大工具库里选出 32 个具有互补路径角色的工具，目标不是 top-k relevance，而是 route completeness。最后 reranker 负责把已选工具排成 producer-before-consumer 的顺序。训练监督来自成功执行轨迹，分别学习 membership、entry、path role、slot order 和 pairwise precedence。\n\n这个设计与常见两阶段检索的区别在于：它显式把“菜单顺序”当成优化对象，而不是检索后附带排序；同时它不改在线 agent 本身，也不增加调用预算，只改执行前的接口。换句话说，论文把性能提升归因尽量收敛到 menu construction，而不是把 agent backbone、planner、budget 一起改掉。","experiments":"从引言和方法部分可确认，实验是在固定 agent、固定 32-tool 预算下，比较 State-Path 菜单与 relevance-based 菜单对终局任务成功率、complete-chain coverage、以及 executable entry 的影响。作者还强调做了跨工具库、跨 evaluator、跨 executor family 的比较，并使用 paired outcomes 和 controlled variants 来说明收益来自路径覆盖和顺序，而不是其他混杂因素。\n\n当前提供的全文片段没有包含结果表和具体数字，因此不能准确写出提升幅度、数据规模或训练细节。即便如此，实验设计的关键信息已经足够清楚：一是控制变量做得比较严，在线 agent loop 和 call budget 不变；二是指标不只看最终成功率，还看菜单是否包含完整链条、首个可执行工具是否足够靠前，这使得方法收益更可解释。若后续阅读全文，建议重点核对三类结果：对长链任务的收益是否显著高于短链任务；对大工具库是否更稳；以及 reranker 单独带来的增益是否接近 retriever，还是主要收益来自覆盖而非排序。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读。先看 Introduction，里面用 receipt/email 的例子把“最终动作相关，但前置工具缺失”讲得很直观。然后看 Methodology 前半部分，重点理解 state path、四种工具角色、以及为什么菜单顺序本身会影响在线执行。再看实验部分的主表和消融，确认收益到底来自 relation-aware retrieval、path-role coverage，还是 producer-before-consumer reranking。\n\n如果只想抓住主线，20-30 分钟够了。若你在做 agent 系统，可以多看附录里关于各阶段可见信息、训练轨迹监督和案例分析的部分，因为这些决定了方法能否迁移到真实 API 生态。对纯 LLM 预训练研究员，不必深挖实现细节，理解它如何把 action-space pruning 变成结构化先验即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical bottleneck for online tool-using agents: before execution starts, which small subset of tools should be shown to the agent, and in what order.
